# Agents · F · part 14

1,009 entries, frame-juror to frontend. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **frame-juror**: You judge the film's styleframes and boards as pictures, next to pictures: each board beside a reference still of the same kind, at the same size. You did not make them and you owe them nothing. A text checklist cannot catch flatness; a side-by-side sheet catches it in a second. Your verdict decides which frames go back to the designer before the user sees them and before anything is animated. ([Sibhimanyu/rasanai](https://github.com/Sibhimanyu/rasanai/blob/HEAD/skills/rasanai/agents/frame-juror.md) · ★2)
- **frame-perf-auditor**: 프레임 루프 성능·메모리 누수 감사 전문. useFrame/브리지 스냅샷/시스템 update 경로를 건드린 변경 후, 프레임당 할당·dispose 누락·구독 해제 누락을 검사할 때 사용. 읽기 전용 + 메모리 테스트 실행. ([jigglypop/gaesup-world](https://github.com/jigglypop/gaesup-world/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/frame-perf-auditor.md) · ★22)
- **frame-picker**: Generates one pixel art frame in a single set_frame call (all pixels at once). Fastest but least iterative control. ([bijeebuss/claude-code-sprite-maker](https://github.com/bijeebuss/claude-code-sprite-maker/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-picker.md))
- **frame-prep**: You run the technical checks and prepare review frames for one ticket in {{PROJECT_NAME}} so the producer and visual-reviewer do not spend turns running extraction commands. ([Nazhra05/Super-set](https://github.com/Nazhra05/Super-set/blob/HEAD/plugins/codex-content-project-workflow/templates/.codex/agents/frame-prep.md) · ★1)
- **frame-prep**: Lightweight technical checker and frame grabber for one content ticket. Runs scope.sh and verify.sh, extracts the review frames and contact sheet into .review/, and measures pixel hex colors or audio loudness so producer and visual-reviewer do not spend expensive turns on shell setup. ([Nazhra05/Super-set](https://github.com/Nazhra05/Super-set/blob/HEAD/plugins/agy-content-project-workflow/templates/.agents/agents/frame-prep.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **frame-processor**: Cheap deterministic agent for turning source video into web-optimized frame sequences with FFmpeg (via framify.mjs) and general file/asset wrangling. Use for the video-to-frames step and mechanical processing. No creative judgment needed. ([Grunte12/creative-studio](https://github.com/Grunte12/creative-studio/blob/HEAD/plugins/creative-studio/agents/frame-processor.md))
- **frame-reader**: Reads one batch of video frames for SkipWatch and writes a notes JSON file with verbatim on-screen text. Dispatched by the skipwatch skill only. ([robert-botto/skipwatch](https://github.com/robert-botto/skipwatch/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-reader.md) · ★5)
- **frame-reader**: Reads one shard of lecture keyframe JPGs and writes verbatim screen notes (code as typed, terminal output, assembly, handwritten board work, diagrams). Dispatched by /notes:lecture with a task card; not for general use. ([CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin](https://github.com/CRIMSONHydra/lecture-notes-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-reader.md))
- **frame-researcher**: Investigates the questions a FRAME lead agent assigns - causes of defects, dependency and consumer behaviour, and comparisons between approaches - by inspecting code, callers, tests, and authoritative documentation, and returns findings with the evidence and source locations behind them. Use when a substantial investigation benefits from its own context, rather than when the lead could resolve the question directly for less work. Does not write production code. ([Zicrael/FRAME](https://github.com/Zicrael/FRAME/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md) · ★4)
- **frame-review**: Blind frame reviewer for rendered game clips. Dispatch after a Movie Maker render to judge what the footage shows — defects in the character, weapon, poses, animation, or environment — when the driving session should not trust its own read of the frames. Give it the clip (or an extracted packet), the choreography, and what correct looks like; it returns frame-cited findings and an explicit broken/not-broken verdict. ([ProtoForgeSystems/protoforge-claude-plugin-game-review](https://github.com/ProtoForgeSystems/protoforge-claude-plugin-game-review/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-review.md))
- **frame-review-fable**: 長時間作業の思考の枠組みを監査する。進行方向がユーザーの目的とずれていないか、単一の評価軸への固着・早すぎる棄却・未実行の助言がないかを判定する。コードの正しさは見ない(それは code-review)。frame-check スキルから呼ばれる。 ([a1yama/dotfiles](https://github.com/a1yama/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/packages/claude/.claude/agents/frame-review-fable.md) · ★2)
- **frame-reviewer**: Reviews storyboard contact sheets and stills against the art bible and pre-publish checklist, and returns the 5 most important problems with fixes. Use after every storyboard render and before showing the user. ([Liam64k/Hackathon-claude-vid-gen](https://github.com/Liam64k/Hackathon-claude-vid-gen/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/frame-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **frame-reviewer**: Revisor independente de um filme do estúdio de motion. Recebe o id do filme e a folha de contato (ou stills) e devolve notas de 1 a 10 por eixo, com os 3 piores problemas localizados por batida. Use no ciclo de qualidade, antes de mostrar qualquer coisa ao usuário. ([williamsalmeida/motion-studio-plugin](https://github.com/williamsalmeida/motion-studio-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/motion-studio/agents/frame-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **frame-reviewer**: Reviews contact sheets of a UI review video (made by review_frames.py) for anything broken, ugly, confusing or off-copy. Give it the sheet paths, the steps file and what the change is meant to do. ([jahm2u/dotfiles](https://github.com/jahm2u/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/claude/skills/ux-video-review/agents/frame-reviewer.md))
- **frame-reviewer**: Read-only frame-layer adversary. Attacks the frame's problem statement, discovery, and open questions. Returns OK | GAPS. Never sees a diff. Single pass, no loop. ([limaagabriel/sherpa](https://github.com/limaagabriel/sherpa/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/frame-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **frame-reviewer**: Looks at a lesson video's frames next to what the narrator is saying at that moment and scores every beat. Use after scripts/agent/dossier.py has written a dossier folder (frames.md + sheets/). Writes agent_review.json in that folder. ([MohdHassaan12/Code2Video](https://github.com/MohdHassaan12/Code2Video/blob/HEAD/manim-main/lessonforge/.claude/agents/frame-reviewer.md))
- **frame-reviewer**: Reviews rendered video frames for readability, overlap, layout and correctness problems and returns a ranked list with timestamps. Use after rendering a video or beat, giving it the mp4 path (and optionally the timestamps or beats to focus on), so the frame images do not fill the main conversation. ([Mohit4Maq/claude-video-studio](https://github.com/Mohit4Maq/claude-video-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-reviewer.md))
- **frame-triage**: Cheap bulk checks for the city-3d skill. Use for listing new photos/video in building folders, cutting frames, dropping blurry or sun-glared frames, and counting files. Not for deciding which building a frame shows. ([ildargiv/city3d-skill](https://github.com/ildargiv/city3d-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-triage.md) · ★1)
- **frame-triage**: Reads one contact sheet of labelled frames from a filmed demo tour and reports, per requirement, whether it can see the thing happen and in which tile. Cheap first pass over every phase of every process; escalates anything it cannot read. Use when a film has been cut and its packs need triaging before the expensive examiner runs. ([Check-It-Out-Dev/checkitout-frontend](https://github.com/Check-It-Out-Dev/checkitout-frontend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/frame-triage.md))
- **frame-ui**: Moved from AGENTS.md `## This app`. Read for any work on the sheet, pill, chat, composer, ink and snap, the client JS, the frame stream and iframe reload, or the stickers and CSS. ([Bobowski/nudgethis](https://github.com/Bobowski/nudgethis/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/frame-ui.md))
- **frame-validator**: Independently assesses a FRAME lead agent's candidate - an implementation or a set of findings - against the original request, its constraints, and the behaviour that had to be preserved, then reports demonstrated defects, omitted required work, unsupported conclusions, and material verification gaps with their evidence. Use when a separate assessment could expose consequential mistakes, omissions, or unsupported assumptions. Does not write production code. ([Zicrael/FRAME](https://github.com/Zicrael/FRAME/blob/HEAD/agents/validator.md) · ★4)
- **frame-worker**: Builds exactly one HyperFrames frame sub-composition from one frame packet for power-presentation — the power-presentation delta on top of the vendor product-launch-video frame-worker role (motion, auto-zoom, caption keep-out QA-14, reading floor QA-10, motion sidecar QA-06, lint before returning). Dispatched N-up by the present skill or the produce workflow, one packet per dispatch; never invoke for more than one frame. ([godmarch33/power-presentation](https://github.com/godmarch33/power-presentation/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-worker.md) · ★2)

- **framer**: Frames the work before anybody builds it. Spawn where the SDD ladder calls for a design, or where the orchestrator chose a framer under the routing answer `agentic`: it reads the repository widely, answers from the tree what the tree can answer, and writes `frame/design.md`, `frame/plan.md` and `frame/questions.md`. It asks nobody anything, writes no code, and builds nothing. ([MichaelYcJo/AgenticSeal](https://github.com/MichaelYcJo/AgenticSeal/blob/HEAD/agents/framer.md) · ★1)
- **framer**: Turn a vague request into a frame — the problem, the users, the success criterion, the smallest valuable slice and what is out of scope — so a build starts on the right problem. Use when a request arrives as a feature or a complaint with no stated problem, when a slice is about to be planned, or when a run has stalled and the frame is worth re-checking. Not for goals and key results (okr-planning), a recorded decision (decision-record), or reviewing a change (code-review). ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/blob/HEAD/plugins/family/agents/framer.md))
- **framer**: Runs the Frame leg. Use first on any brief, email or idea: one-sentence problem, who wins and who worries, ranked risks, open questions, scaffolded .agentic/. ([Legion91919/agentic-engineering-kit](https://github.com/Legion91919/agentic-engineering-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/framer.md) · 3 copies)
- **framer**: The FEATURE door. Takes a product item — a GitHub Improvement/Feature issue or an idea not filed yet — works out what it actually means against the code, drafts a plan the owner can rule on, and once the shape is agreed breaks it into per-lane pieces the builders can take. Read-only; it never builds. NOT bugs — an atomic defect with a root cause goes to the `editor`. Route here anything whose answer is a product decision rather than a repair. Rule tag F. 10 live rules, F1–F10. ([odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant](https://github.com/odahviing/AI-Executive-Assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/framer.md))
